# Live uPD6383GF EQ biquad trace -- KN5000 IC311, PARAMETRIC EQ, SMALL SEED, 2026-09-11.
# Companion to the SEEDED (0.125..0.5) trace: here UPD6383_BIQSEED=8 right-shifts the
# band-0 state seeds by 7 (to 0x2000/0x4000/0x6000/0x8000) so band 0's biquad output does
# NOT saturate at the makeup/store -- letting the acc->datum STORE constant be decoded.
#
# REGENERATE (binary built with the BIQSEED value-shift, upd6383.cpp; needs MAME -log):
#   cd ~/compartilhado/kn7000_mame_build
#   NAV=1 TYPEIDX=15 AUDIO=key BOOTGATE=19 DWELL=25 DSPVAL=3 UPD6383_SPEC=4000000000 \
#   UPD6383_BIQSEED=8 UPD6383_TRACE_FRAME=300000 \
#   timeout 120 ./kn7000 kn5000 -rompath ./roms -skip_gameinfo -log \
#     -autoboot_script ~/compartilhado/kn7000_mame/tools/rigs/kn5000_dsp_frame_trace.lua
#   sed 's/^\[:dsp1\] //' error.log > this-file   # -log routes logerror to error.log
#
# DECODE (dsp/tools/store_constant_probe.py): band 0 store acc=4842334080, stored to the
# band-1 input cell 0x68 as 0x1209D=73885 = 4842334080>>16 EXACTLY (only shift 16 works)
# => the STORE constant is acc >> ACC_SHIFT(16).  Later bands' acc grows (KN5000 EQ coeffs
# Jury-unstable in this DF-I order, Phase 2) so band 0 is the clean witness.
#
